Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the spin framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 844. The resulting operator achieves bound O(736/n^1).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_844(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 844. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^736(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the spin object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 296. The moduli space has dimension at most 17 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 296 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spin structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 17. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
